# International school admission in Saudi Arabia (KSA Guide Study Here)

Use this when users ask about school admission, enrollment, registration, required documents, placement tests, or how to apply to an international school.

## Typical admission process (most international / private schools)
1. Book a school tour or open day (optional but common).
2. Submit an online or in-person application / enquiry form.
3. Provide required documents (passports, Iqama/ID, birth certificate, vaccines, previous reports).
4. Child takes a diagnostic / placement test and/or interview (KG often uses a readiness interview).
5. School reviews previous records and seat availability.
6. Offer is issued; family pays joining / registration / seat-reservation fee.
7. Student is registered on the Ministry of Education Noor system; term fees are paid.

## Documents usually required
- Student and parents passport copies
- Student and parents Iqama / National ID (Saudis: family card)
- Birth certificate
- Vaccination record
- Previous school report cards (attested by MOE / Saudi embassy if from abroad)
- Transfer / leaving certificate and Noor transfer (if moving from a Saudi school)
- Financial clearance from previous private school (when applicable)
- Passport-size photos
- Sponsor letter for some non-Saudi applicants

## Extra tips
- Grade placement is often based on academic level, not age alone.
- KG usually needs toilet training and ability to follow simple instructions.
- Joining fees (often 2,000–3,000 SAR) may be required to start testing/admission.
- Tuition is commonly quoted excluding 15% VAT for non-Saudis; confirm with the school.
- Always confirm current seats, fees, and document lists with the school — data in the app may change.

### AL-Raed international school
Riyadh, Al Wadi · American · co-ed ·  · fees 16000–26000 SAR/year
Contact: 966920012991,966114709040
Admission steps:
- Submit an online enquiry / registration form to the school
- Placement test and/or interview (KG: readiness interview)
- Submit required documents
- Receive offer and pay registration / seat-reservation fee
- Student registered on the Ministry of Education Noor system and first-term fees paid
Documents required:
- Student & parents' passport copies
- Student & parents' Iqama / National ID (Saudis: family card)
- Birth certificate
- Vaccination record
- Previous school report cards (attested by MOE / Saudi embassy if from abroad)
- Transfer / leaving certificate and Noor transfer (if moving from a Saudi school)
- Financial clearance from previous private school
- Passport-size photos
School notes:
- Submitting an official registration application through the school's administration or available communication channels.
- Attaching a copy of the student's birth certificate or ID.
- Presenting the student's academic record or most recent school certificate.
- Passing a personal interview or academic assessment if required by the school.
- Compliance with the specified age group for each educational stage.
- Completing all required fields in the registration form.
- Paying registration fees in accordance with the school's approved financial regulations.
- Availability of vacant seats in the requested grade.

School notes:
The school does not follow a selective admission system; any student with a desire to learn can be accepted.
Grade placement is determined based on: Academic level (not age alone).
Kindergarten (KG) Requirements:
- No academic level is required.
- A simple interview with the child is conducted.
- The child must be:
- Toilet trained.
- Able to speak and follow simple instructions.Requirements for Higher Grades:
- Taking a Diagnostic Test in core subjects.
- The purpose of the test: to measure the student's level and determine the appropriate grade.
- The student may be required to:
- Attend remedial lessons or support programs.
- Or enroll in a summer program if improvement is needed.

Enrollment for Transfer Students: For students from within Riyadh: pass the admission test, bring the file from the previous school, and transfer the file through the Noor program. For students from outside Riyadh: same procedures, along with copies of ID, passports, and original financial clearance documents.

First — Age requirement for Kindergarten and First Grade Elementary: Admission to Kindergarten (KG1 – KG2 – KG3) or First Grade Elementary is determined based on the student's date of birth, in accordance with the age categories approved by the school for each grade level.
Second — Documents required for registration:
- A copy of the birth certificate (original must be presented for verification).

Contact: 966555730069,966920012991
Admission steps:
- Submit an online enquiry / registration form to the school
- Placement test and/or interview (KG: readiness interview)
- Submit required documents
- Receive offer and pay registration / seat-reservation fee
- Student registered on the Ministry of Education Noor system and first-term fees paid
Documents required:
- Student & parents' passport copies
- Student & parents' Iqama / National ID (Saudis: family card)
- Birth certificate
- Vaccination record
- Previous school report cards (attested by MOE / Saudi embassy if from abroad)
- Transfer / leaving certificate and Noor transfer (if moving from a Saudi school)
- Financial clearance from previous private school
- Passport-size photos
School notes:
- The student's age must be appropriate for the academic stage applied for, in accordance with the Saudi Ministry of Education's regulations.
- Students must pass placement tests and a personal interview to assess their various skills and academic level.
- The following documents must be submitted: the student's most recent academic certificates, vaccination record, valid ID copies for both the student and parent/guardian, a copy of the original birth certificate, and a receipt confirming payment of all tuition fees.
- The registration form available on the Al-Mutaqaddimah Schools Company website must be completed, including the relevant details of the parent/guardian and student, such as full name, email address, and mobile phone number.

School notes:

- Transfer / leaving certificate and Noor transfer (if moving from a Saudi school)
- Financial clearance from previous private school
- Passport-size photos
School notes:
Admission requirements at Dar Al Bra'ah Global Schools:
- Enrollment: Through a personal interview and an academic level test in both Arabic and English.
- First Grade (Elementary): Minimum age of 5 years and 9 months.
- Subsequent Grades: Submission of proof of passing the previous stage.
- Middle and High School: A minimum academic grade of "Very Good" and passing the personal interview.
- Foreign Certificates: Certificates must be authenticated and equivalated by the competent authorities.
- Kindergarten: Level determined through tests and specific registration documents.
- Required Documents: Previous stage certificates, ID card, and medical certificate.Age Compliance: The minimum age specified for each educational stage must be respected.
